---
description: How SESAR 1.0 classification values map to material type.
icon: right-left
---

# Classification to material type

SESAR 1.0 stored **material** (top-level classification) and optional **classification** as a more-specific value. Migrated samples use [material type](renamed-vocabularies.md#material-types-was-material-and-classification) from the current list. Top-level material maps to **general material type**; classification maps to one or more **material types**.

The original values are kept as **legacy top-level classification** and **legacy classification**.

API registration uses the **material type** leaf label. The form shows the **material type path**. When one legacy value maps to more than one material type, or a material type has more than one parent path, the extra values appear as line breaks within the same table row.

| SESAR 1.0 | Material type | Material type path |
